      if (this.isCurrent(session)) void this.bootstrapTape(session);
    }, delay);
  }

  /**
   * Whether a REST row is tape the stream already delivered. Inside a live
   * span (open or closed) it is; at a span's first or last millisecond the
   * aggregate's fill ids decide: wholly before the first live trade or wholly
   * after the last one it is missing tape and is kept, wholly within it is
   * dropped, and an aggregate straddling the boundary cannot be told apart,
   * so it is dropped and that millisecond is marked unverified: no coverage
   * claim includes it.
   */
  private admitRestRow(session: Session, row: AggTradeRow): boolean {
    const spans: readonly LiveSpan[] = session.live === null
      ? session.liveSpans
      : [...session.liveSpans, { from: session.live.from, to: Infinity, firstId: session.live.firstId, lastId: Infinity }];
    for (const span of spans) {
      if (row.T > span.from && row.T < span.to) return false;
      if (row.T !== span.from && row.T !== span.to) continue;
      // At a boundary millisecond, the fill ids place the aggregate: wholly
      // before the span's first trade (only at its first millisecond) or
      // wholly after its last (only at its last) is missing tape; wholly
      // between the two is the stream's; anything else straddles. A span of
      // one millisecond is both boundaries at once.
      const before = row.T === span.from && row.l < span.firstId;
      const after = row.T === span.to && row.f > span.lastId;
      if (before || after) continue;
      const within = row.f >= span.firstId && row.l <= span.lastId;
      if (within) return false;
      this.markUnverified(session, row.T);
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  private markUnverified(session: Session, ms: number): void {
    if (!session.unverified.includes(ms)) session.unverified.push(ms);
  }

  /**
   * One validated page into the retention and the pool, as the runtime's
   * warm-up seeds a storage chunk: rows from the calibration window (the
   * newest fifteen minutes at the bootstrap's start) calibrate the floor
   * (`calibrate: true`, every size counted), older rows are admitted at the
   * floor; rows the stream already delivered are left out. Returns the rows kept.
   */
  private seedTape(session: Session, rows: readonly AggTradeRow[]): number {
    const calibration: TradeFrame[] = [];
    const older: TradeFrame[] = [];
    let oldestTs = Infinity;
    for (const row of rows) {
      if (!this.admitRestRow(session, row)) continue;
      const trade = aggTradeFrame(session.selection, row);
      if (row.T < oldestTs) oldestTs = row.T;
      (row.T >= session.calibrationFrom ? calibration : older).push(trade);
      this.offerMarker(session, trade, false);
    }
    if (oldestTs === Infinity) return 0;
    if (calibration.length > 0) session.pool.seed(calibration, { fromMs: oldestTs, calibrate: true });
    if (older.length > 0) session.pool.seed(older, { fromMs: oldestTs });
    return calibration.length + older.length;
  }

  /** The newest hole between two observed stretches (the open stream span counts) that is neither given up on nor made only of unverified milliseconds. */
  private nextSeamGap(session: Session): TapeInterval | null {
    const intervals = this.coverageIntervals(session);
    for (let i = intervals.length - 1; i >= 1; i -= 1) {
      const gap: TapeInterval = { from: intervals[i - 1].to, to: intervals[i].from, endAggId: intervals[i - 1].endAggId, endTradeId: intervals[i - 1].endTradeId };
      if (session.unfillable.some((open) => open.from === gap.from && open.to === gap.to)) continue;
      const interior = gap.to - gap.from - 1;
      const unverifiedInside = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms > gap.from && ms < gap.to).length;
      if (interior <= unverifiedInside) continue;
      return gap;
    }
    return null;
  }

  /**
   * Closes the holes the tape has: between the bootstrap's newest row and the
   * stream's first trade, or between one stream's last trade and the next
   * stream's first. Kline recovery repairs bars, not trades, so the prints of
   * such a hole are read from REST within a bounded budget: by id from the
   * row after the last REST row when the hole follows one, else by time from
   * the hole's first millisecond, every row reconciled against the live tape
   * by fill id. A hole that cannot be closed stays a hole and is never inside
   * a completeness claim. One fill runs at a time; when nothing is left to
   * fill and a snapshot is waiting, it goes out.
   */
  private fillNextSeam(session: Session): void {
    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || session.seam.inFlight || session.seam.timer !== null) return;
    const gap = this.nextSeamGap(session);
    if (gap === null) {
      if (session.seam.publishWhenSettled) this.publishTapeState(session);
      return;
    }
    void this.fillSeam(session, gap);
  }

  private async fillSeam(session: Session, gap: TapeInterval): Promise<void> {
    const seam = session.seam;
    seam.inFlight = true;
    seam.gap = gap;
    let closed = false;
    let endAggId = gap.endAggId;
    try {
      let fromId: number | null = gap.endAggId === undefined ? null : gap.endAggId + 1;
      for (let pages = 0; pages < MAX_SEAM_FILL_PAGES; pages += 1) {
        const rows = await this.fetchAggTrades(session, fromId === null ? { startTime: gap.from } : { fromId }, seam);
        if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
        const inside = rows.filter((row) => row.T <= gap.to);
        if (inside.length > 0) {
          this.seedTape(session, inside);
          endAggId = inside[inside.length - 1].a;
        }
        // The hole is closed when a row past its end was read, or the page
        // was short (nothing newer existed on the exchange at the read).
        if (inside.length < rows.length || rows.length < TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) {
          closed = true;
          break;
        }
        fromId = rows[rows.length - 1].a + 1;
      }
    } catch (error) {
      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
      seam.inFlight = false;
      seam.gap = null;
      seam.failures += 1;
      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam fill failed", error);
      if (seam.failures < SEAM_FILL_FAILURES_BEFORE_GIVING_UP) {
        const delay = seam.backoffMs;
        seam.backoffMs = Math.min(seam.backoffMs * 2, STREAM_BACKOFF_CAP_MS);
        seam.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
          seam.timer = null;
          this.fillNextSeam(session);
        }, delay);
        return;
      }
      seam.failures = 0;
      seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
      this.fillNextSeam(session);
      return;
    }
    seam.inFlight = false;
    seam.gap = null;
    seam.failures = 0;
    seam.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
    if (closed) {
      addInterval(session.observed, { from: gap.from, to: gap.to, endAggId });
      // Coverage grew: the snapshot says so.
      session.seam.publishWhenSettled = true;
    } else {
      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: a tape seam is wider than a fill may walk; it stays open");
      session.unfillable.push({ from: gap.from, to: gap.to });
    }
    this.fillNextSeam(session);
  }
}

   477	/** The stretches with the given milliseconds cut out (an unverified boundary millisecond splits the stretch around it). */
   478	function subtractMilliseconds(intervals: TapeInterval[], milliseconds: readonly number[]): TapeInterval[] {
   479	  let result = intervals;
   480	  for (const ms of milliseconds) {
   481	    const next: TapeInterval[] = [];
   482	    for (const interval of result) {
   483	      if (ms < interval.from || ms > interval.to) {
   484	        next.push(interval);
   485	        continue;
   486	      }
   487	      if (ms > interval.from) next.push({ from: interval.from, to: ms - 1, startTradeId: interval.startTradeId });
   488	      if (ms < interval.to) next.push({ from: ms + 1, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId });
   489	    }
   490	    result = next;
   491	  }
   492	  return result;
   493	}

   516	/** The runtime's request validation (aggressive-history.js parseAggressiveHistoryRequest), minus the exact-key check the worker already applies. */
   517	export function parseAggressiveHistoryRequest(msg: Record<string, unknown>, nowMs: number): AggressiveHistoryRequest | null {
   518	  const { request_id: requestId, from_ts: fromMs, to_ts: toMs, min_size: minSize, cursor } = msg;
   519	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(requestId) || (requestId as number) < 0) return null;
   520	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(fromMs) || (fromMs as number) < 0) return null;
   521	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(toMs) || (toMs as number) <= (fromMs as number)) return null;
   522	  if ((toMs as number) > nowMs + AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_FUTURE_SKEW_MS) return null;
   523	  if ((toMs as number) - (fromMs as number) > MAX_AGGRESSIVE_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS) return null;
   524	  if (typeof minSize !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(minSize) || minSize < 0) return null;
   525	  if (cursor !== undefined && (typeof cursor !== "string" || cursor.length === 0 || cursor.length > MAX_HISTORY_CURSOR_LENGTH || !/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(cursor))) return null;
   526	  return { requestId: requestId as number, fromMs: fromMs as number, toMs: toMs as number, minSize, cursor: typeof cursor === "string" ? cursor : null };
   527	}

   529	function base64UrlEncode(text: string): string | null {
   530	  try {
   531	    return btoa(text).replace(/\+/g, "-").replace(/\//g, "_").replace(/=+$/, "");
   532	  } catch {
   533	    return null;
   534	  }
   535	}
   536	
   537	function base64UrlDecode(token: string): string | null {
   538	  try {
   539	    const padded = token.replace(/-/g, "+").replace(/_/g, "/") + "=".repeat((4 - (token.length % 4)) % 4);
   540	    return atob(padded);
   541	  } catch {
   542	    return null;
   543	  }
   544	}

   546	/** A page cursor bound to its request (as the runtime's: version, instrument, window, floor, position), so a stale or foreign one is refused. */
   547	export function encodeHistoryCursor(selection: Selection, request: AggressiveHistoryRequest, last: { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string }): string | null {
   548	  const scope = selection.provider === "BINANCE" ? selection.marketKind : selection.assetClass;
   549	  const token = base64UrlEncode(JSON.stringify({
   550	    v: HISTORY_CURSOR_VERSION, s: selection.symbol, k: scope, f: request.fromMs, t: request.toMs, m: request.minSize, x: last.ts_exchange, i: last.trade_id,
   551	  }));
   552	  return token !== null && token.length <= MAX_HISTORY_CURSOR_LENGTH ? token : null;
   553	}
   554	
   555	export function decodeHistoryCursor(token: string, selection: Selection, request: AggressiveHistoryRequest): { ts_exchange: number; trade_id: string } | null {
   556	  const text = base64UrlDecode(token);
   557	  if (text === null) return null;
   558	  let decoded: unknown;
   559	  try {
   560	    decoded = JSON.parse(text);
   561	  } catch {
   562	    return null;
   563	  }
   564	  if (!isRecord(decoded)) return null;
   565	  const scope = selection.provider === "BINANCE" ? selection.marketKind : selection.assetClass;
   566	  if (
   567	    decoded.v !== HISTORY_CURSOR_VERSION || decoded.s !== selection.symbol || decoded.k !== scope ||
   568	    decoded.f !== request.fromMs || decoded.t !== request.toMs || decoded.m !== request.minSize ||
   569	    !Number.isSafeInteger(decoded.x) || (decoded.x as number) < request.fromMs || (decoded.x as number) >= request.toMs ||
   570	    typeof decoded.i !== "string" || decoded.i.length === 0
   571	  ) {
   572	    return null;
   573	  }
   574	  return { ts_exchange: decoded.x as number, trade_id: decoded.i };
   575	}

   577	function bookLevels(raw: unknown): Array<{ price: number; size: number }> | null {
   578	  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return null;
   579	  const levels: Array<{ price: number; size: number }> = [];
   580	  for (const level of raw) {
   581	    if (!Array.isArray(level) || level.length < 2) return null;
   582	    const price = finiteNumber(level[0]);
   583	    const size = finiteNumber(level[1]);
   584	    if (price === undefined || size === undefined || price <= 0 || size < 0) return null;
   585	    if (size === 0) continue;
   586	    levels.push({ price, size });
   587	  }
   588	  return levels;
   589	}

   591	export class BrowserBinanceTransport implements TransportSocket {
   592	  readyState = CONNECTING;
   593	  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
   594	  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null = null;
   595	  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null = null;
   596	  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
   597	
   598	  readonly url: string;
   599	  private readonly fetchImpl: typeof fetch;
   600	  private readonly WebSocketImpl: new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket;
   601	  private readonly now: () => number;
   602	  private readonly setTimer: (callback: () => void, ms: number) => unknown;
   603	  private readonly clearTimer: (handle: unknown) => void;
   604	  private readonly endpoints: BrowserBinanceEndpoints;
   605	  private session: Session | null = null;
   606	  private openTimer: unknown = null;
   607	  /** The worker opted in to marker_history frames (its `capabilities` message), as the runtime remembers per client. */
   608	  private markerHistoryOptIn = false;
   609	
   610	  constructor(url: string, options: BrowserBinanceTransportOptions = {}) {
   611	    this.url = url;
   612	    this.fetchImpl = options.fetch ?? ((input, init) => fetch(input, init));
   613	    this.WebSocketImpl = options.WebSocket ?? (WebSocket as unknown as new (url: string) => UpstreamSocket);
   614	    this.now = options.now ?? (() => Date.now());
   615	    this.setTimer = options.setTimeout ?? ((callback, ms) => setTimeout(callback, ms));
   616	    this.clearTimer = options.clearTimeout ?? ((handle) => clearTimeout(handle as ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>));
   617	    this.endpoints = { ...DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS, ...(options.endpoints ?? {}) };
   618	    // A WebSocket opens asynchronously; so does this, so the worker installs
   619	    // its handlers before anything fires.
   620	    this.openTimer = this.setTimer(() => {
   621	      this.openTimer = null;
   622	      if (this.readyState !== CONNECTING) return;
   623	      this.readyState = OPEN;
   624	      this.onopen?.(new Event("open"));
   625	      // The runtime pushes its current pair on connect; with no selection
   626	      // from the worker we do the same with the catalog's first spot pair.
   627	      if (this.session === null && this.readyState === OPEN) this.select(defaultSelection());
   628	    }, 0);
   629	  }
   630	
   631	  /** Closed bars held back while a gap fill is pending (diagnostics and tests). */
   632	  get heldClosedBarCount(): number {
   633	    return this.session?.heldClosedBars.size ?? 0;
   634	  }
   635	
   636	  /** Sizes of the tape coverage bookkeeping (diagnostics and tests). */
   637	  get tapeCoverage(): { observed: number; liveSpans: number; unfillable: number; unverified: number; staged: number } {
   638	    const session = this.session;
   639	    if (session === null) return { observed: 0, liveSpans: 0, unfillable: 0, unverified: 0, staged: 0 };
   640	    return { observed: session.observed.length, liveSpans: session.liveSpans.length, unfillable: session.unfillable.length, unverified: session.unverified.length, staged: session.tape.staged.length };
   641	  }
   642	
   643	  send(data: string): void {
   644	    if (this.readyState !== OPEN) throw new DOMException("transport is not open", "InvalidStateError");
   645	    let msg: unknown;
   646	    try {
   647	      msg = JSON.parse(data);
   648	    } catch {
   649	      return;
   650	    }
   651	    if (!isRecord(msg) || typeof msg.type !== "string") return;
   652	    if (msg.type === "select") {
   653	      const selection = parseSelectRequest(msg);
   654	      if (selection !== null) this.select(selection);
   655	      return;
   656	    }
   657	    if (msg.type === "capabilities") {
   658	      // As the runtime: opting in gets the current marker set at once (nothing
   659	      // when there is none yet; the bootstrap sends it when it completes).
   660	      const optIn = msg.marker_history === true;
   661	      const session = this.session;
   662	      this.markerHistoryOptIn = optIn;
   663	      if (optIn && session !== null) this.setTimer(() => this.deliverMarkerHistory(session), 0);
   664	      return;
   665	    }
   666	    const reply = this.replyFor(msg);
   667	    if (reply !== null) this.setTimer(() => this.deliver(reply), 0);
   668	  }
   669	
   670	  close(): void {
   671	    if (this.readyState === CLOSED) return;
   672	    this.readyState = CLOSED;
   673	    if (this.openTimer !== null) {
   674	      this.clearTimer(this.openTimer);
   675	      this.openTimer = null;
   676	    }
   677	    this.endSession();
   678	  }
   679	
   680	  /** The contract's own "not available" answer for a request this transport cannot serve; null when the contract defines none. */
   681	  private replyFor(msg: Record<string, unknown>): Record<string, unknown> | null {
   682	    const session = this.session;
   683	    if (session === null) return null;
   684	    const identity = identityFields(session.selection);
   685	    const echo = (keys: readonly string[]): Record<string, unknown> | null => {
   686	      const fields: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   687	      for (const key of keys) {
   688	        if (msg[key] === undefined) return null;
   689	        fields[key] = msg[key];
   690	      }
   691	      return fields;
   692	    };
   693	    switch (msg.type) {
   694	      case "aggressive_history":
   695	        return this.aggressiveHistoryReply(session, msg);
   696	      case "delta_profile": {
   697	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "row_width"]);
   698	        if (fields === null) return null;
   699	        return { type: "delta_profile", ...identity, ...fields, complete: false, reason: "STORAGE_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, pending: 0, rows: [] };
   700	      }
   701	      case "band_history": {
   702	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi", "column_ms"]);
   703	        if (fields === null) return null;
   704	        return { type: "band_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, row_lo: null, row_hi: null, complete: false, reason: "GEOMETRY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, columns: [] };
   705	      }
   706	      case "wall_history": {
   707	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi"]);
   708	        if (fields === null) return null;
   709	        return { type: "wall_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, complete: false, reason: "HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, walls: [], next: null };
   710	      }
   711	      case "l2_event_history": {
   712	        const fields = echo(["request_id", "from_ts", "to_ts", "price_lo", "price_hi"]);
   713	        if (fields === null) return null;
   714	        return { type: "l2_event_history", ...identity, ...fields, row_width: null, complete: false, reason: "HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE", retry_after_ms: null, trades: [], wall_trades: [], trades_held_from: null, next: null };
   715	      }
   716	      case "profile_config":
   717	      case "profile_history":
   718	        return { type: "analytics_status", ...identity, indicator: "PROFILE", status: "UNAVAILABLE" };
   719	      default:
   720	        // capabilities, liquidity_resync, l2_resync, exhaustion_history,
   721	        // initiative_history: the contract defines no reply shape, so the
   722	        // indicators keep their own "no frame yet" state.
   723	        return null;
   724	    }
   725	  }
   726	
   727	  private deliver(frame: Record<string, unknown>): void {
   728	    if (this.readyState !== OPEN) return;
   729	    const data = JSON.stringify(frame);
   730	    const event = typeof MessageEvent === "function"
   731	      ? new MessageEvent("message", { data })
   732	      : ({ data } as unknown as MessageEvent);
   733	    this.onmessage?.(event);
   734	  }
   735	
   736	  private endSession(): void {
   737	    const session = this.session;
   738	    if (session === null) return;
   739	    this.session = null;
   740	    session.closed = true;
   741	    session.abort?.abort();
   742	    session.abort = null;
   743	    if (session.timer !== null) {
   744	      this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   745	      session.timer = null;
   746	    }
   747	    session.tape.abort?.abort();
   748	    session.tape.abort = null;
   749	    if (session.tape.timer !== null) {
   750	      this.clearTimer(session.tape.timer);
   751	      session.tape.timer = null;
   752	    }
   753	    session.seam.abort?.abort();
   754	    session.seam.abort = null;
   755	    if (session.seam.timer !== null) {
   756	      this.clearTimer(session.seam.timer);
   757	      session.seam.timer = null;
   758	    }
   759	    if (session.tape.graceTimer !== null) {
   760	      this.clearTimer(session.tape.graceTimer);
   761	      session.tape.graceTimer = null;
   762	    }
   763	    this.closeStream(session);
   764	    this.keepTapeState(session);
   765	  }
   766	
   767	  /** Keeps the pair's tape state for the next instance or the next selection of the pair; the oldest pair goes when too many are kept. */
   768	  private keepTapeState(session: Session): void {
   769	    if (session.selection.provider !== "BINANCE") return;
   770	    const key = tapeStateKey(session.selection);
   771	    tapeStates.delete(key);
   772	    tapeStates.set(key, {
   773	      markers: session.markers,
   774	      pool: session.pool,
   775	      observed: session.observed,
   776	      liveSpans: session.liveSpans,
   777	      unfillable: session.unfillable,
   778	      unverified: session.unverified,
   779	      calibrationFrom: session.calibrationFrom,
   780	      recentTrades: session.recentTrades,
   781	      done: session.tape.done,
   782	    });
   783	    while (tapeStates.size > MAX_TAPE_STATES) {
   784	      const oldest = tapeStates.keys().next().value;
   785	      if (oldest === undefined) break;
   786	      tapeStates.delete(oldest);
   787	    }
   788	  }
   789	
   790	  /**
   791	   * The upstream stream was lost after delivering trades: the tick store on
   792	   * the main thread has a live span open that must end where the trades
   793	   * stopped. A lost runtime socket does exactly that through the worker's
   794	   * close and reconnect, so this transport closes the same way; the pair's
   795	   * tape state is kept for the instance the worker creates next, which
   796	   * reconciles the hole from REST or leaves it outside every claim.
   797	   */
   798	  private lostUpstream(session: Session): void {
   799	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   800	    console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: the stream was lost after delivering trades; closing so the worker reconnects");
   801	    this.close();
   802	    const event = typeof CloseEvent === "function"
   803	      ? new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" })
   804	      : ({ code: 1006, reason: "upstream stream lost" } as unknown as CloseEvent);
   805	    this.onclose?.(event);
   806	  }
   807	
   808	  private closeStream(session: Session): void {
   809	    if (session.connectTimer !== null) {
   810	      this.clearTimer(session.connectTimer);
   811	      session.connectTimer = null;
   812	    }
   813	    // The tape this stream delivered is observed up to its last live trade;
   814	    // the prints between that and the next stream's first trade are a hole,
   815	    // which a seam fill closes from REST or which stays open.
   816	    const live = session.live;
   817	    if (live !== null) {
   818	      session.liveSpans.push({ from: live.from, to: live.last, firstId: live.firstId, lastId: live.lastId });
   819	      addInterval(session.observed, { from: live.from, to: live.last, startTradeId: live.firstId, endTradeId: live.lastId });
   820	      session.live = null;
   821	    }
   822	    const stream = session.stream;
   823	    session.stream = null;
   824	    if (stream === null) return;
   825	    stream.onopen = null;
   826	    stream.onmessage = null;
   827	    stream.onclose = null;
   828	    stream.onerror = null;
   829	    try {
   830	      stream.close();
   831	    } catch {
   832	      void 0;
   833	    }
   834	  }
   835	
   836	  private isCurrent(session: Session): boolean {
   837	    return this.readyState === OPEN && this.session === session && !session.closed;
   838	  }
   839

  1284	  private routeStreamMessage(session: Session, parsed: unknown): void {

  1287	    if (payload.e === "kline") {
  1288	      const bar = parseKlineEvent(payload.k);
  1289	      if (bar === null) return;
  1290	      if (bar.closed && bar.openTime <= session.lastClosedOpen) return;
  1291	      if (session.recovering) {
  1292	        if (bar.closed && !session.fillInFlight && session.awaitingBoundary === bar.openTime && bar.openTime === session.lastClosedOpen + BAR_INTERVAL_MS) {
  1293	          // The minute the fill could not yet confirm: the stream's close is final.

  1302	            this.scheduleRetry(session, () => {
  1303	              if (this.recoveryIsLive(session, session.streamGeneration)) void this.recoverAfterGap(session, session.streamGeneration);
  1304	            });
  1305	          }

  1307	        }
  1308	        if (bar.closed) {

  1326	      const size = finiteNumber(payload.q);

  1343	      }

  1347	      // As the runtime's handleEnvelope: the pool records every new trade, and
  1348	      // a print the retention admits goes out as a marker right behind it.

  1350	      this.offerMarker(session, trade, true);

  1353	      if (opensSpan) {

  1378	  // ---- Tape-derived frames: markers, the aggressive pool, their history ----

  1380	  /** Offers a print to the runtime's large-trade retention; an admitted one is broadcast as a `marker` when asked. */
  1381	  private offerMarker(session: Session, trade: TradeFrame, broadcast: boolean): void {
  1382	    const marker = markerOf(trade);
  1383	    if (session.markers.insertAll([marker]) && broadcast) this.deliver(marker);
  1384	  }
  1385	
  1386	  /** The newest markers the runtime would still hold: within its seven-day window, oldest first, the newest 5,000 for a snapshot. */
  1387	  private retainedMarkers(session: Session): MarkerFrame[] {
  1388	    const oldestKept = this.now() - MARKER_HISTORY_WINDOW_MS;
  1389	    return session.markers.snapshot().filter((marker) => marker.ts_exchange >= oldestKept);
  1390	  }
  1391	
  1392	  /**
  1393	   * Forgets coverage older than the pool's retention (nothing older can be
  1394	   * claimed: the pool has no print for it) and bounds every list, so
  1395	   * reconnects over a long session cannot grow them without end. The gap a
  1396	   * seam fill is working on, and the stretches beside it, are kept.
  1397	   */
  1398	  private pruneCoverage(session: Session): void {
  1399	    const cutoff = this.now() - POOL_WINDOW_MS;
  1400	    const active = session.seam.gap;
  1401	    const keepsActive = (interval: TapeInterval): boolean =>
  1402	      active !== null && (interval.to === active.from || interval.from === active.to || (interval.from === active.from && interval.to === active.to));
  1403	    const clip = (interval: TapeInterval): TapeInterval =>
  1404	      interval.from >= cutoff ? interval : { from: cutoff, to: interval.to, endTradeId: interval.endTradeId, endAggId: interval.endAggId };
  1405	    session.observed = session.observed.filter((interval) => interval.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(interval)).map(clip);
  1406	    session.liveSpans = session.liveSpans.filter((span) => span.to >= cutoff);
  1407	    session.unfillable = session.unfillable.filter((gap) => gap.to >= cutoff || keepsActive(gap));
  1408	    session.unverified = session.unverified.filter((ms) => ms >= cutoff);
  1409	    if (session.observed.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.observed.splice(0, session.observed.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
  1410	    if (session.liveSpans.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.liveSpans.splice(0, session.liveSpans.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
  1411	    if (session.unfillable.length > MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES) session.unfillable.splice(0, session.unfillable.length - MAX_COVERAGE_ENTRIES);
  1412	    if (session.unverified.length > MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS) session.unverified.splice(0, session.unverified.length - MAX_UNVERIFIED_MS);
  1413	  }
  1414	
  1415	  /**
  1416	   * Every stretch of tape observed in full: the closed ones, plus the open
  1417	   * stream span up to its last trade (a trade in flight may still arrive, so
  1418	   * the observed tape ends at the last print seen, never in the future),
  1419	   * merged where they touch, with every unverified boundary millisecond cut
  1420	   * out.
  1421	   */
  1422	  private coverageIntervals(session: Session): TapeInterval[] {
  1423	    this.pruneCoverage(session);
  1424	    const intervals = session.observed.map((interval) => ({ ...interval }));
  1425	    if (session.live !== null) addInterval(intervals, { from: session.live.from, to: session.live.last, startTradeId: session.live.firstId, endTradeId: session.live.lastId });
  1426	    return subtractMilliseconds(intervals, session.unverified);
  1427	  }
  1428	
  1429	  /** Where the stretch of complete tape that reaches the stream's last trade begins, or null while no stream delivers. */
  1430	  private coverageNowFrom(session: Session): number | null {
  1431	    const live = session.live;
  1432	    if (live === null) return null;
  1433	    const open = this.coverageIntervals(session).find((interval) => interval.from <= live.last && interval.to >= live.last);
  1434	    return open === undefined ? null : open.from;
  1435	  }
  1436	
  1437	  /**
  1438	   * What the snapshot may claim: the pool's prints, its floor, and coverage
  1439	   * from the start of the continuous observed stretch that reaches now (never
  1440	   * before the pool's own window), or from now itself, an empty claim, while
  1441	   * no stream delivers or a hole separates the stream from what was read.
  1442	   */
  1443	  private poolCoverage(session: Session): { prints: readonly TradeFrame[]; floor: number; from: number } {
  1444	    const snapshot = session.pool.snapshot();
  1445	    const nowMs = this.now();
  1446	    const from = this.coverageNowFrom(session);
  1447	    return { prints: snapshot.prints, floor: snapshot.floor, from: from === null ? nowMs : Math.min(nowMs, Math.max(snapshot.from, from)) };
  1448	  }
  1449	
  1450	  /** The runtime's snapshot for the pair as this transport knows it: every closed bar, the last 100 live trades, the newest markers, the print pool. */
  1451	  private snapshotFrame(session: Session): Record<string, unknown> {
  1452	    const bars = [...session.closedBars.values()].sort((a, b) => a.openTime - b.openTime);
  1453	    const markers = this.retainedMarkers(session);
  1454	    const pool = this.poolCoverage(session);
  1455	    return {
  1456	      type: "snapshot",
  1457	      ...identityFields(session.selection),
  1458	      bars: bars.map((row) => barFrame(session.selection, row, true, row.reconstructed)),
  1459	      trades: [...session.recentTrades],
  1460	      markers: markers.slice(-MAX_SNAPSHOT_MARKERS),
  1461	      vpin_status: "DISABLED",
  1462	      ...(session.tape.done || session.observed.length > 0 || session.live !== null
  1463	        ? { aggressive_prints: [...pool.prints], aggressive_prints_floor: pool.floor, aggressive_prints_from: pool.from }
  1464	        : {}),
  1465	    };
  1466	  }
  1467	
  1468	  /** The full marker set as marker_history frames, 5,000 rows each, `reset` on the first, as the runtime's markerHistoryFrames. */

  1470	    if (!this.isCurrent(session) || !this.markerHistoryOptIn) return;

  1472	    if (markers.length === 0) return;

  1486	  /** After the bootstrap or a seam fill: the snapshot again (as the runtime re-broadcasts it after a warm-up chunk), the forming bar, and the marker history for a client that opted in. */

  1496	  /**

  coverHistory(fromMs: number, toMs: number, minSize: number): void {
    if (!Number.isFinite(fromMs) || !Number.isFinite(toMs) || toMs < fromMs || !Number.isFinite(minSize)) return;
    let span: HistoryCoverage = { from: fromMs, to: toMs, floor: minSize };
    const apart: HistoryCoverage[] = [];

      if (entry.floor !== minSize || entry.to < span.from || entry.from > span.to) apart.push(entry);
      else span = { from: Math.min(span.from, entry.from), to: Math.max(span.to, entry.to), floor: minSize };
    }
    apart.push(span);
    apart.sort((a, b) => a.from - b.from || a.floor - b.floor);

  }

  subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void {
    this._listeners.add(listener);
    return () => {
      this._listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  }

  private _notify(): void {

    this._tallyDirty = false;
    this._generation += 1;
    this._coverage = [];
    this._historyWantedFrom = null;
    this._heldFrom = -Infinity;
    this._liveSince = null;
    this._liveSpans = [];
    this._lastLiveTs = -Infinity;
    this._liveDroppedThrough = -Infinity;
    this._historyVersion += 1;
    this._lastAsk = null;
    this._givenUp = [];
    for (const trade of trades) this._push(toPrint(trade), false);
    this._prints.sort(comparePrints);
    this._trim();
    this._notify();
  }

  absorb(trades: readonly TradeFrame[]): void {
    // A (re)connect: the stretch recorded so far stays complete up to its last live print.
    if (this._liveSince !== null && this._lastLiveTs > this._liveSince) {
      this._liveSpans.push({ from: this._liveSince, to: this._lastLiveTs });
      if (this._liveSpans.length > MAX_LIVE_SPANS) this._liveSpans.splice(0, this._liveSpans.length - MAX_LIVE_SPANS);
    }
    this._liveSince = this._now();
    let taken = 0;
    for (const trade of trades) {
      if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
      const print = toPrint(trade);
      this._push(print);
      this._fold(print);
      taken += 1;
    }
    this._trim();
    if (taken > 0 || this._prints.length === 0) this._notify();
  }

  add(trade: TradeFrame): void {
    if (this._liveSince === null) this._liveSince = this._now();
    if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) return;
    const print = toPrint(trade);
    this._push(print);
    this._fold(print);
    this._trim();
    this._notify();
  }

  addHistory(trades: readonly TradeFrame[], thinBeforeMs = -Infinity): number {
    let taken = 0;
    for (const trade of trades) {
      if (this._ids.has(trade.trade_id)) continue;
      this._push(toPrint(trade, true), false);
      taken += 1;
    }
    if (taken === 0) return 0;
    this._prints.sort(comparePrints);
    if (thinBeforeMs > -Infinity) this._thin(thinBeforeMs);
    this._trim();
    this._autoFloorCount = -1;
    this._generation += 1;
    this._historyVersion += 1;
    this._tallyDirty = true;
    this._settleUntil = this._now() + HISTORY_SETTLE_MS;
    this._notify();
    return taken;
  }

  private _push(print: AggressivePrint, insertLate = true): void {
    print.seq = this._seq;
    this._seq += 1;
    if (insertLate && print.ts < this._lastTs) {
      let lo = 0;
      let hi = this._prints.length;
      while (lo < hi) {
        const mid = (lo + hi) >>> 1;
        if (this._prints[mid].ts <= print.ts) lo = mid + 1;
        else hi = mid;
      }
      this._prints.splice(lo, 0, print);
      // The incremental cluster and absorption trackers have already seen a
      // later print, so they must derive their state from the ordered tape.
      this._generation += 1;
      this._tallyDirty = true;
    } else {
      this._prints.push(print);
    }
    this._ids.add(print.id);
    if (!print.history) this._liveCount += 1;
    if (!print.history && print.ts > this._lastLiveTs) this._lastLiveTs = print.ts;
    if (print.ts > this._lastTs) this._lastTs = print.ts;
  }

  private _drop(print: AggressivePrint): void {
    this._ids.delete(print.id);
    if (!print.history) this._liveCount -= 1;
    if (!print.history && print.ts > this._liveDroppedThrough) this._liveDroppedThrough = print.ts;
  }

  private _thin(beforeMs: number): void {
    const buckets = new Map<string, AggressivePrint[]>();
    for (const print of this._prints) {
      if (print.ts >= beforeMs) break;
      const key = `${Math.floor(print.ts / HISTORY_THIN_BUCKET_MS)}:${print.side}`;
      const bucket = buckets.get(key);
      if (bucket) bucket.push(print);
      else buckets.set(key, [print]);
    }
    const gone = new Set<AggressivePrint>();
    for (const bucket of buckets.values()) {
      if (bucket.length <= HISTORY_THIN_PER_MINUTE_SIDE) continue;
      bucket.sort((a, b) => b.size - a.size || a.ts - b.ts || (a.id < b.id ? -1 : a.id > b.id ? 1 : 0));
      for (const print of bucket.slice(HISTORY_THIN_PER_MINUTE_SIDE)) gone.add(print);
    }
    if (gone.size === 0) return;
    for (const print of gone) this._drop(print);
    this._prints = this._prints.filter((print) => !gone.has(print));
  }

  private _trim(): void {
    if (this._prints.length < MAX_TAPE_PRINTS + TRIM_SLACK_PRINTS) return;
    let excess = this._prints.length - MAX_TAPE_PRINTS;
    const keepFrom = this._coverage.length === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(...this._coverage.map((entry) => entry.floor));

import type {
  AggressivePrint,
  AggressiveTape,
} from "../indicators/aggressive-orders/aggressiveTape";

/**
 * The terminal's own L1 print store, as an indicator sees it.
 *
 * `AggressiveTape` holds every print of the live pair -- fed the moment it lands
 * -- plus whatever history the server could serve for before this browser
 * connected (a bounded, **floor-filtered** pool, not the raw tape). It is the
 * same store the whale detector and Aggressive Order Flow read.
 *
 * Because history is served above a size floor and the live tape is complete,
 * a window is either covered by every print (`"all"`), covered only by prints
 * above that floor (`"thin"`), or not covered at all (`"none"`). An indicator
 * that prices volume has to say which one it is looking at rather than assume.
 */
export type TickCoverage = "all" | "thin" | "none";

export interface IndicatorTicks {
  /** Every print the store still holds, oldest first. */
  readonly prints: readonly AggressivePrint[];
  /** The instant complete live recording began, or null when nothing is live yet. */
  readonly liveSince: number | null;
  /** How the store covers `[fromTs, toTs)`. */
  coverageOf(fromTs: number, toTs: number): TickCoverage;
}

/** The index of the first print at or after `ts`, or `prints.length`. */
export function firstPrintAtOrAfter(
  prints: readonly AggressivePrint[],
  ts: number,
): number {
  let low = 0;
  let high = prints.length;
  while (low < high) {
    const middle = (low + high) >> 1;
    if (prints[middle].ts < ts) low = middle + 1;
    else high = middle;
  }
  return low;
}

export function sumPrintsIn(
  prints: readonly AggressivePrint[],
  fromTs: number,
  toTs: number,
): { volume: number; priceVolume: number; priceSquaredVolume: number } {
  let volume = 0;
  let priceVolume = 0;
  let priceSquaredVolume = 0;
  for (let index = firstPrintAtOrAfter(prints, fromTs); index < prints.length; index += 1) {
    const print = prints[index];
    if (print.ts >= toTs) break;
    const { price, size } = print;
    if (!Number.isFinite(price) || price <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(size) || size <= 0) continue;
    volume += size;
    priceVolume += price * size;
    priceSquaredVolume += price * price * size;
  }
  return { volume, priceVolume, priceSquaredVolume };
}

function coversWindow(
  intervals: readonly { from: number; to: number }[],
  fromTs: number,
  toTs: number,
): boolean {
  return intervals.some(
    (interval) => interval.from <= fromTs && interval.to >= toTs,
  );
}

export function indicatorTicksOf(tape: AggressiveTape | null): IndicatorTicks | null {
  if (tape === null) return null;
  return {
    get prints(): readonly AggressivePrint[] {
      return tape.prints;
    },
    get liveSince(): number | null {
      return tape.liveSince;
    },
    coverageOf(fromTs: number, toTs: number): TickCoverage {
      // Every print from the moment live recording began; the window's end may
      // be in the future while the bar is still forming, which is still covered.
      // An earlier live stretch (before a reconnect) covers what lies inside it.
      // Either is complete only past the newest live print the store's size cap
      // dropped: a window it reached lost prints, so it reads as thin.
      const liveFrom = tape.liveSince;
      const live =
        (liveFrom !== null && fromTs >= liveFrom) ||
        tape.liveSpans.some((span) => span.from <= fromTs && span.to >= toTs);
      if (live) return fromTs > tape.liveDroppedThrough ? "all" : "thin";
      // A floor of zero is the server saying it served this stretch in full.
      if (coversWindow(tape.historyIntervalsAt(0), fromTs, toTs)) return "all";
      if (coversWindow(tape.historyIntervalsAt(Infinity), fromTs, toTs))
        return "thin";
      return "none";
    },
  };
}

// and from the REST bootstrap of recent aggregate trades, the aggressive-print
// pool on the snapshot, aggressive_history answered from the pool, marker_history
// for a client that opted in, and the honest states when the bootstrap fails.
// The bootstrap pages come from the same 10,000 recorded BTC-USDT aggregate
// trades the parity test drives the runtime modules with, so the markers on
// the wire are compared with the runtime's own history buffer here as well.

const recorded = loadRecordedTrades();
const FIRST_ID = Number(recorded[0].trade_id.slice(1));

const OLDEST_TS = recorded[0].ts_exchange;
const NEWEST_TS = recorded[recorded.length - 1].ts_exchange;
// "now" is a few seconds after the newest recorded trade, inside the forming minute.
const NOW_MS = NEWEST_TS + 5_000;
const MINUTE = Math.floor(NOW_MS / 60_000) * 60_000;
const SPOT = { type: "select", symbol: "BTC-USDT", provider: "BINANCE", marketKind: "SPOT" };

/** The mirror's aggTrades row for a recorded trade. */
function aggTradeRow(trade: TradeFrame): Record<string, unknown> {
  const id = Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1));
  return { a: id, p: String(trade.price), q: String(trade.size), f: id, l: id, T: trade.ts_exchange, m: trade.side === "sell", M: true };
}

/** Serves a tape as the mirror would: the newest page without parameters, the 1,000 ids from `fromId`, or the first 1,000 rows at or after `startTime`. */
function recordedPage(url: string, rows: readonly TradeFrame[] = recorded): Record<string, unknown>[] {
  const params = new URL(url).searchParams;
  const fromId = params.get("fromId");
  const startTime = params.get("startTime");
  if (startTime !== null) return rows.filter((trade) => trade.ts_exchange >= Number(startTime)).slice(0, TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE).map(aggTradeRow);
  const first = Number(rows[0].trade_id.slice(1));
  const last = Number(rows[rows.length - 1].trade_id.slice(1));
  const start = fromId === null ? Math.max(first, last - TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE + 1) : Number(fromId);
  return rows.filter((trade) => {
    const id = Number(trade.trade_id.slice(1));
    return id >= start && id < start + TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE;
  }).map(aggTradeRow);
}

function historyRequest(requestId: number, fromTs: number, toTs: number, minSize: number, cursor?: string) {
  return { type: "aggressive_history", request_id: requestId, from_ts: fromTs, to_ts: toTs, min_size: minSize, ...(cursor === undefined ? {} : { cursor }) };
}

/** Sends one aggressive_history request and returns its reply (or null when the transport drops it). */
async function askHistory(h: Harness, request: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<Extract<ServerFrame, { type: "aggressive_history" }> | null> {
  const before = h.frames.length;
  h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(request));
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
  const reply = h.parsed().slice(before).find((frame) => frame.type === "aggressive_history");
  return reply === undefined ? null : reply as Extract<ServerFrame, { type: "aggressive_history" }>;
}

const klineRow = (openTime: number, close: string) => [openTime, "79500.00", "79600.00", "79400.00", close, "5.000", openTime + 59_999, "0", 50, "2.000", "0", "0"];
const klines = [klineRow(MINUTE - 120_000, "79510.00"), klineRow(MINUTE - 60_000, "79520.00"), klineRow(MINUTE, "79530.00")];

class FakeUpstreamSocket implements UpstreamSocket {
  static instances: FakeUpstreamSocket[] = [];
  readyState = 0;
  onopen: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
  onmessage: ((event: MessageEvent) => void) | null = null;
  onclose: ((event: CloseEvent) => void) | null = null;
  onerror: ((event: Event) => void) | null = null;
  readonly url: string;
  constructor(url: string) {
    this.url = url;
    FakeUpstreamSocket.instances.push(this);
  }
  close(): void {
    this.readyState = 3;
  }
  open(): void {
    this.readyState = 1;
    this.onopen?.(new Event("open"));
  }
  message(payload: unknown): void {
    this.onmessage?.(new MessageEvent("message", { data: JSON.stringify(payload) }));
  }
  drop(): void {
    this.readyState = 3;
    this.onclose?.(new CloseEvent("close", { code: 1006 }));
  }
}

type Harness = {
  transport: BrowserBinanceTransport;
  frames: Record<string, unknown>[];
  parsed: () => ServerFrame[];
  aggTradesMock: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn>;
  stream: () => FakeUpstreamSocket;
  now: () => number;
};

/** The instance the worker creates after a transport closed: the same mirror, the same clock, the pair's kept tape state. */
function reconnected(previous: Harness): Harness {
  const previousAggTrades = previous.aggTradesMock as unknown as (url: string) => Promise<Response>;
  const next = harness((url) => previousAggTrades(url), previous.now);
  next.aggTradesMock = previous.aggTradesMock;
  return next;
}

/** A hand-built mirror row with explicit fill ids. */
function aggRow(a: number, T: number, f: number, l: number, q = "5.00000"): Record<string, unknown> {
  return { a, p: "79530.00", q, f, l, T, m: false, M: true };
}

function jsonResponse(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
}

function harness(aggTrades: (url: string) => Promise<Response> = async (url) => jsonResponse(recordedPage(url)), now: () => number = () => NOW_MS): Harness {
  FakeUpstreamSocket.instances = [];
  const aggTradesMock = vi.fn(aggTrades);
  const transport = new BrowserBinanceTransport("ws://127.0.0.1:8000/ws", {
    fetch: ((url: string) => (url.includes("/aggTrades") ? aggTradesMock(url) : Promise.resolve(jsonResponse(klines)))) as unknown as typeof fetch,
    WebSocket: FakeUpstreamSocket,
    now,
  });
  const frames: Record<string, unknown>[] = [];
  transport.onmessage = (event) => {
    frames.push(JSON.parse(event.data as string) as Record<string, unknown>);
  };
  return {
    transport,
    frames,
    parsed: () => frames.map((frame) => {
      const parsed = parseServerFrame(frame, { nowMs: now() });
      if (parsed === null) throw new Error(`frame rejected by parseServerFrame: ${JSON.stringify(frame).slice(0, 400)}`);
      return parsed;
    }),
    aggTradesMock,
    stream: () => FakeUpstreamSocket.instances.at(-1)!,
    now,
  };
}

async function openAndSelect(h: Harness, { markerHistory = true } = {}): Promise<void> {
  h.transport.onopen = () => {
    if (markerHistory) h.transport.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "capabilities", marker_history: true }));
    h.transport.send(JSON.stringify(SPOT));
  };
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
  expect(h.transport.readyState).toBe(1);
}

/** Lets the sequential REST pages of the bootstrap resolve (each page is a few promise hops). */
async function settle(): Promise<void> {
  for (let i = 0; i < 200; i += 1) await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(0);
}

/** Lets a bootstrap read its pages and, with a stream connecting or open but silent, wait out the grace before committing them. */
async function settleBootstrap(): Promise<void> {
  await settle();
  await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(BOOTSTRAP_COMMIT_GRACE_MS);
  await settle();
}

function liveTrade(id: number, ts: number, size: string, sell: boolean, price = "79530.00") {
  return { stream: "btcusdt@trade", data: { e: "trade", E: ts + 5, s: "BTCUSDT", t: id, p: price, q: size, T: ts, m: sell, M: true } };
}

function runtimeMarkers(trades: readonly TradeFrame[]): MarkerFrame[] {
  const buffer = createHistoryBuffer({ markerBucketMs: MARKER_BUCKET_MS, maxMarkersPerBucketSide: MARKERS_PER_BUCKET_SIDE });
  for (const trade of trades) buffer.recordMarker({ ...trade, type: "marker" });
  return buffer.snapshotMarkers();
}

describe("BrowserBinanceTransport tape bootstrap", () => {
  it("pages recent aggregate trades from the mirror and re-broadcasts a snapshot whose markers and pool pass the parser and equal the runtime's", async () => {
    const h = harness();
    await openAndSelect(h);
    await settleBootstrap();

   425	  const price = finiteNumber(raw.p);
   426	  const size = finiteNumber(raw.q);
   427	  if (price === undefined || size === undefined || price <= 0 || size <= 0) return null;
   428	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.a) || (raw.a as number) < 0 || !Number.isSafeInteger(raw.T) || (raw.T as number) < 0 || typeof raw.m !== "boolean") return null;
   429	  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(raw.f) || !Number.isSafeInteger(raw.l) || (raw.f as number) < 0 || (raw.l as number) < (raw.f as number)) return null;
   430	  return { a: raw.a as number, p: price, q: size, f: raw.f as number, l: raw.l as number, T: raw.T as number, m: raw.m };
   431	}
   432	
   433	/**
   434	 * One REST page, validated as a whole: every row well formed, no more rows
   435	 * than asked, ids consecutive (Binance numbers aggregate trades without gaps
   436	 * per symbol) and, for a page asked by `fromId`, starting at it. A page that
   437	 * fails any of this is a failed read, never a shorter page: a dropped row
   438	 * would otherwise end the walk early and leave a hole inside claimed coverage.
   439	 */
   440	export function parseAggTradesPage(body: unknown, fromId: number | null): AggTradeRow[] {
   441	  if (!Array.isArray(body)) throw new Error("aggTrades: body is not an array");
   442	  if (body.length > TAPE_BOOTSTRAP_PAGE) throw new Error("aggTrades: more rows than asked");
   443	  const rows: AggTradeRow[] = [];
   444	  for (const raw of body) {
   445	    const row = parseAggTradeRow(raw);
   446	    if (row === null) throw new Error("aggTrades: malformed row");
   447	    rows.push(row);
   448	  }
   449	  rows.sort((a, b) => a.a - b.a);
   450	  for (let i = 1; i < rows.length; i += 1) {
   451	    if (rows[i].a !== rows[i - 1].a + 1) throw new Error("aggTrades: page is not id-contiguous");
   452	    if (rows[i].T < rows[i - 1].T) throw new Error("aggTrades: page is not in time order");
   453	  }
   454	  if (fromId !== null && rows.length > 0 && rows[0].a !== fromId) throw new Error("aggTrades: page does not start at fromId");
   455	  return rows;
   456	}
   457	
   458	/** Merges a closed stretch into the list, joining stretches that overlap or touch (adjacent milliseconds); the ends keep the ids of whichever stretch reaches furthest. */
   459	function addInterval(intervals: TapeInterval[], added: TapeInterval): void {
   460	  if (!(added.to >= added.from)) return;
   960	    if (session.timer !== null) this.clearTimer(session.timer);
   961	    session.timer = this.setTimer(() => {
   962	      session.timer = null;
   963	      if (this.isCurrent(session)) action();
   964	    }, delayMs);
   965	  }
   966	
   967	  private restFailureReason(session: Session): string {
   968	    return session.selection.provider === "BINANCE" && session.selection.marketKind === "PERPETUAL"
   969	      ? HEALTH_REASON_PERPETUAL_UNREACHABLE
   970	      : HEALTH_REASON_REST_FAILED;
   971	  }
   972	
   973	  private async bootstrap(session: Session): Promise<void> {
   974	    let rows: KlineRow[];
   975	    const confirmedBefore = this.now() - CLOSE_CONFIRMATION_MARGIN_MS;
   976	    try {
   977	      rows = await this.fetchKlines(session, { limit: SNAPSHOT_BARS + 1 });
   978	    } catch (error) {
   979	      if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   980	      console.warn("browserBinanceTransport: kline history request failed", error);
   981	      this.deliverHealth(session, "UNAVAILABLE", this.restFailureReason(session));
   982	      this.scheduleRetry(session, () => void this.bootstrap(session));
   983	      return;
   984	    }
   985	    if (!this.isCurrent(session)) return;
   986	    const now = this.now();
   987	    // Only minutes that had closed before the request went out are final; the
   988	    // minute at the edge may have been served partial and is shown forming
   989	    // until the stream's reconcile confirms it.
   990	    const closedRows = rows.filter((row) => row.closeTime < confirmedBefore).slice(-SNAPSHOT_BARS);
   991	    const forming = rows.find((row) => row.closeTime >= confirmedBefore && row.openTime <= now);
   992	    const identity = identityFields(session.selection);
   993	    session.lastClosedOpen = closedRows.length > 0 ? closedRows[closedRows.length - 1].openTime : -1;
   994	    session.closedBars = new Map(closedRows.map((row) => [row.openTime, { ...row, reconstructed: true }]));
   995	    // The connect snapshot: the closed bars, and the pair's markers, recent
   996	    // trades and pool when its tape state was kept from an earlier instance
   997	    // (as the runtime's connect snapshot carries what its buffers hold).
   998	    this.deliver(this.snapshotFrame(session));
   999	    session.lastFormingBar = null;
  1000	    session.heldClosedBars.clear();
  1001	    session.heldFormingBar = null;
  1002	    session.recovering = false;
  1003	    session.fillInFlight = false;
  1004	    session.awaitingBoundary = null;
  1005	    session.holeRetries = 0;
  1006	    session.degraded = false;
  1007	    session.bookLive = false;
  1008	    this.deliverHealth(session, "READY", this.readyReason(session));
  1009	    this.deliver({ type: "book", ...identity, status: "UNAVAILABLE", reason: BOOK_REASON_STREAM_CONNECTING });
  1010	    if (forming !== undefined) this.deliverFormingBar(session, forming);
  1011	    // A kept tape state has its markers already: a client that opted in gets them now, as on the runtime's connect.
  1012	    if (session.tape.done) this.deliverMarkerHistory(session);
  1013	    session.backoffMs = STREAM_BACKOFF_INITIAL_MS;
  1014	    this.openStream(session);
  1015	    this.startTapeBootstrap(session);
  1016	  }
  1017	
  1018	  /** The READY reason: plain, or saying that the tape bootstrap failed and markers start at connect. */

codex
I reproduced a false completeness claim: reconnect spans ending at millisecond 1000 and starting at 1001 merge without a REST fill, even when their fill IDs skip from 100 to 200. I also found that switching pairs during the bootstrap grace period can discard staged pages while caching the bootstrap as complete.

codex
1. **WARNING — `apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:460–470, 1422–1426`**
   Coverage merges overlapping or adjacent milliseconds without verifying fill-ID continuity. Reproduced: one stream ends at timestamp 1000 with ID 100; the next starts at 1001 with ID 200. Coverage merges into one stretch and `nextSeamGap()` returns `null`, although fills 101–199 may be missing. `aggressive_history` can consequently report incomplete tape as complete.
   
   **Fix:** verify ID continuity or reconcile through REST before merging independently observed spans, including spans sharing a millisecond. Add regression tests for both cases.

2. **WARNING — `apps/web/src/workers/browserBinanceTransport.ts:772–781, 892–895`**
   Cached `done` means the bootstrap finished reading, even if its pages remain staged during the two-second grace period. Switching pairs then discards those pages. Returning restores `started: true`, `done: true`, and no staged pages, permanently skipping that bootstrap. Reproduced this restored state.
   
   **Fix:** persist staged pages and resume their commit, or cache completion only after they are committed so returning restarts the read. Test switching away during grace and returning.
